## Topics
- [Off-Road Autonomous Driving](#off-road-autonomous-driving): End-to-end autonomy, perception, decision-making, and planning for off-road driving systems.
- [Off-Road Navigation and Path Planning](#off-road-navigation-and-path-planning): Navigation, path planning, motion planning, and trajectory generation in off-road or cross-country environments.
- [Off-Road Freespace and Traversability](#off-road-freespace-and-traversability): Freespace detection, terrain traversability estimation, and drivable-area reasoning for unstructured terrain.
- [Unstructured Environment Autonomous Navigation](#unstructured-environment-autonomous-navigation): Autonomous navigation and planning in rough, complex, or weakly structured outdoor environments.
- [VLA for Autonomous Driving](#vla-for-autonomous-driving): Vision-language-action models and action generation methods for autonomous driving.
- [VLA for Off-Road Driving](#vla-for-off-road-driving): VLA-style policies and multimodal action reasoning applied to off-road or unstructured driving scenes.
- [VLM for Navigation and Localization](#vlm-for-navigation-and-localization): Vision-language models, visual foundation models, localization, and map-aware navigation.
- [Visual Prompt Reasoning](#visual-prompt-reasoning): Visual prompting and reasoning methods that may support planning, navigation, and driving decisions.
- [World Models for Autonomous Driving](#world-models-for-autonomous-driving): World models, future prediction, planning priors, and trajectory reasoning for driving and navigation.
- [Embodied AI for Navigation](#embodied-ai-for-navigation): Embodied agents, embodied intelligence, and robot navigation methods relevant to autonomous systems.
- [Off-Road Datasets and Benchmarks](#off-road-datasets-and-benchmarks): Datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation protocols for off-road driving and unstructured environments.
- [Scene Captioning for Driving](#scene-captioning-for-driving): Scene captioning, multimodal routing, and language-conditioned scene understanding for driving.
